## `AFG-8510-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Role:** Allergen-Cross-Contact Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Kindergarten Playground Rubber Surfacing & Toy Safety EN71

- **Intro:** Early-Childhood-Screening Specialist — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Early Childhood Interactive Whiteboard Classroom — Verify Power Isolation and LOTO: Voltage reading must be 0 VAC ±0.5 VAC at the outlet and at the equipment power input.
